That is a critical flaw in the file rotation logic that I missed, but your analysis of the data flow caught it perfectly! \n\nBecause we read RSS feeds from newest to oldest, and because the system blindly appends to the last partially filled file from a previous run, it will inevitably inject brand new articles at the very bottom of a file containing old articles. This completely scrambles the chronology!\n\nI have created an `implementation_plan.md` to permanently solve this issue. {"step_index":1286,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-31T08:44:22Z","content":"I have investigated both of those sites, and their scripts are working perfectly, but you have uncovered another hidden layer of security!\n\nHere is exactly why BusinessDay only hit 47 and DailyTrust hit 399: **Cloudflare Rate-Limiting**.\n\nBoth of those sites use Cloudflare. When our script paginates backward (`?paged=1`, `?paged=2`, `?paged=3`...)
